# AgriShield 360° — Raw Source Evidence (ICAR 2022–2026)

This document provides verbatim, source-grounded evidence extracted directly from the four ICAR Annual Reports (2022–23, 2023–24, 2024–25, and 2025–26). No values have been estimated, inferred, or supplemented from external literature.

**Total Extracted Evidence Blocks:** 699

---

Crop: Rice
Variety: Sahyadri Panchmukhi
Information type: Variety Release & Salient Features
Extracted information: Area: Karnataka | Salient features: Suitable for lowland situation in kharif season, yield 50–55 q/ha, maturity 130– 135 days, tolerant to leaf and neck blast apart from tolerance to major insect- pests, stem-borer, gall midge and leaf folder. | Maturity: 130-135 days
Report: ICAR Annual Report 2022-23
Year: 2022-23
Page: 27
Section: Crop Improvement - Rice Varieties Released and Notified

CROPS Crop varieties released and notified Since 1965, more than 6,000 improved field crops varieties (6,226) have been developed which include  3,059  of cereals, 1,021 of oilseeds, 1,136 of pulses, 250 of forage crops, 553 of fiber crops, 157 of period, a total of 283 varieties/hybrids have been notified and released for commercial cultivation, which include 35 biofortified varieties and 32 varieties to combat the various abiotic stresses like drought, water scarcity, flood, waterlogging, salinity, sodicity, low temperature etc. Use of precision breeding tools, i.e. marker assisted selection has also been done in breeding 10 traits specific varieties. The details of these varieties are given below: Cereals: One hundred and twenty-five high yielding varieties/hybrids of cereals comprising 47 of rice, 21 of in different agro-ecologies of the country. | Maturity: 125-125 days
